# GDAC SUPER BOWL LX: 1,455 ANALYTICAL FINDINGS

**Team AI4U | Game Day Analytics Challenge 2026**
**Dataset:** 46,257 cleaned tweets | 38,552 unique users | 59 brands | 54 languages | ~3.7 hours
**Total Findings:** 1455

### Finding #1: Ro_1 -- Volume Rank #1
**Description:** Ro_1 generated 1,861 tweets, capturing 4.0% of total conversation.
**Stats:** Tweet count: 1,861 | Share: 4.0% | Unique users: 1,786
**Explanation:** Top-tier volume brand dominating conversation. User-to-tweet ratio: 1.04 tweets per unique user.

## 13. Yell Index (ALL-CAPS Behavior)
*59 findings*

### Finding #709: Amazon Ring_1 -- Yelling WORKS
**Description:** ALL-CAPS tweets outperform for Amazon Ring_1. 20 tweets (1.6%) had >60% uppercase.
**Stats:** Caps tweets: 20 (1.6%) | Caps avg likes: 242.2 | Normal avg likes: 10.4 | Lift: +2239.2%
**Explanation:** The audience rewards high-energy, urgent-tone content. Emotional intensity matches brand identity.

### Finding #710: Base44_1 -- Yelling HURTS
**Description:** Yelling hurts engagement for Base44_1. 0 tweets (0.0%) had >60% uppercase.
**Stats:** Caps tweets: 0 (0.0%) | Caps avg likes: 0.0 | Normal avg likes: 4.1 | Lift: +0.0%

## 15. Media Effect (Visual Content)
*59 findings*

### Finding #827: Amazon Ring_1 -- Visuals Hurt
**Description:** Text-only tweets outperform visuals for Amazon Ring_1. 230 tweets (18.1%) included images/video.
**Stats:** Media tweets: 230 (18.1%) | Media avg WES: 99.9 | Text-only avg WES: 2623.6 | WES lift: -96.2% | RT lift: -96.3%
**Explanation:** This audience responds to the message itself, not visual packaging. Text-only authenticity wins.

**Description:** Visual content boosts engagement for Base44_1. 37 tweets (39.8%) included images/video.
**Stats:** Media tweets: 37 (39.8%) | Media avg WES: 4.3 | Text-only avg WES: 0.6 | WES lift: +583.9% | RT lift: +575.6%

## 16. Tweet Length Sweet Spot

### Finding #886: Amazon Ring_1 -- Best Length: Medium (50-150 chars)
**Description:** Amazon Ring_1 audience responds best to medium (50-150 chars) tweets.
**Stats:** Short (<50) avg WES: 76.4 (144 tweets) | Medium (50-150) avg WES: 2609.3 (1049 tweets) | Long (150+) avg WES: 10.1 (76 tweets)
**Explanation:** Optimal length is medium (50-150 chars) with 2609.3 avg WES. Medium-length provides enough context without losing attention.

## 17. Emoji Effect
*59 findings*

### Finding #945: Amazon Ring_1 -- Emojis Hurt Engagement
**Description:** Plain-text tweets outperform emoji tweets for Amazon Ring_1.
**Stats:** Emoji tweets: 204 (16.1%) | Emoji avg WES: 112.9 | No-emoji avg WES: 2559.5 | Lift: -95.6%
**Explanation:** Emoji usage dilutes message seriousness for this audience.

## 19. Mention (@) Effect
*59 findings*

### Finding #1063: Amazon Ring_1 -- Mentions Boost Engagement
**Description:** Tweets tagging other users outperform for Amazon Ring_1.
**Stats:** Mention tweets: 1075 (84.7%) | Mention avg WES: 2556.0 | No-mention avg WES: 6.4 | Lift: +39766.8%
**Explanation:** Tagging draws attention from other audiences.

### Finding #1064: Base44_1 -- Mentions Reduce Engagement
**Description:** Standalone tweets outperform mention-heavy tweets for Base44_1.
**Stats:** Mention tweets: 82 (88.2%) | Mention avg WES: 2.0 | No-mention avg WES: 3.0 | Lift: -33.3%
**Explanation:** Mentions scatter attention -- focused standalone messages resonate more.

## 21. Global Dataset Overview
*7 findings*

### Finding #1240: Total Dataset Size
**Description:** The cleaned dataset contains 46,257 tweets from 38,552 unique users across 59 brands.
**Stats:** Tweets: 46,257 | Users: 38,552 | Brands: 59 | Languages: 54
**Explanation:** Cleaned from 65,186 raw tweets (29% removal rate via bot detection and deduplication).

### Finding #1241: Total Engagement Generated
**Description:** The entire dataset generated 28,047,162 total WES, 385,213 likes, 139,986,337 retweets, 31,174 replies, 2,051 quotes, and 23,643 bookmarks.
**Stats:** Total WES: 28,047,162 | Likes: 385,213 | RT: 139,986,337 | Replies: 31,174 | Quotes: 2,051 | Bookmarks: 23,643
**Explanation:** All engagement happened within a ~3.7-hour window, making this one of the most concentrated engagement events in social media.

### Finding #1242: Total Impressions
**Description:** Combined impression count across all tweets: 19,044,635.
**Stats:** Total impressions: 19,044,635 | Avg per tweet: 412
**Explanation:** Represents total eyeballs across the entire conversation.

### Finding #1243: Average Tweet Profile
**Description:** The average tweet was 115 characters, 18.9 words, and scored 606.3 WES.
**Stats:** Avg length: 115 chars | Avg words: 18.9 | Avg WES: 606.3 | Median WES: 40.2
**Explanation:** Mean-to-median WES gap of 566.1 indicates heavy right-skew -- a small number of viral tweets massively inflate the average.

### Finding #1244: Content Type Breakdown
**Description:** 68.0% of tweets were retweets, 20.8% were replies, and the remainder were original tweets.
**Stats:** Retweets: 68.0% | Replies: 20.8% | Original: 11.2% | With media: 21.3% | With emoji: 23.7% | With questions: 7.0%
**Explanation:** The dominance of retweets (68%) confirms that Super Bowl conversation is primarily amplification, not creation.

### Finding #1245: Language Diversity
**Description:** Tweets spanned 54 languages. English was dominant but not exclusive.
**Stats:** Languages: 54 | Top: en (77.0%)
**Explanation:** 54 languages in a 3.7-hour US sporting event demonstrates the global reach of Super Bowl advertising.

### Finding #1246: Peak Minute of Activity
**Description:** The single busiest minute was minute 16 with 16,870 tweets.
**Stats:** Peak minute: 16 | Peak tweets: 16,870 | Avg tweets/min: 771
**Explanation:** Demonstrates the extreme burst nature of live-event social media.
